        Idea:
P1 Foundation: typed Handle scaffold + custom-node lifecycle/lockfile + RunPod smoke-test scaffold.

This is the combined first phase of implementing the Python composition DSL designed in `docs/python_composition_dsl_plan.md` (recently revised — read the current version) and addressing the issues called out in `docs/python_composition_dsl_review.md`. It folds together two originally-separate efforts (MP-2 typed Handles, MP-4 lifecycle/lockfile) plus a RunPod-based smoke-test scaffold so the whole thing can ship as one squashable PR.

Two later phases will build on this output: (a) MP-3 will expand the block library and add the compile-time serialization gate; (b) MP-5 will introduce VibeFlow multi-stage orchestration. Do NOT preempt their scope.

DELIVERABLES — all must land:

### A. Typed Handle scaffold (closes review §1, §2, §3, §10)

A1. `Handle` dataclass — frozen, fields: `node_id` (str), `output_slot` (str | int), `output_type` (str | None — populated later by MP-6 schema integration), `name` (str | None).
   - `__str__` returns `f"{node_id}.{output_slot}"` matching the existing string-ref convention (see `vibecomfy/blocks/subgraph.py:47` precedent and `vibecomfy/workflow.py:128-132` consumer that splits on `.`).
   - `__eq__` matches BOTH another Handle (by node_id+slot) AND its string form, so `handle == "12.0"` works without manual `str()`. Closes the silent type-erasure hole.
   - `__hash__` consistent with `__eq__`.

A2. Tighten `Handles` (plural wrapper at `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:11-35`) — change value type from `Mapping[str, Any]` to `Mapping[str, Handle]`. Update construction sites in `vibecomfy/blocks/` (notably `subgraph.py`) to wrap string refs as Handle instances. Backward-compat: callers passing raw strings must be coerced with a `DeprecationWarning` once per call site.

A3. `wf.node(class_type, **kwargs)` — typed authoring wrapper over `VibeWorkflow.add_node(...)` (`vibecomfy/workflow.py:122-126`). Returns an object with `.out(name_or_index) -> Handle` and `.id` (matches GraphBuilder's `id=` convention). Per the revised plan's API Concepts, this is the canonical authoring entry point. Does NOT promise mypy-grade safety; `T` parameterization deferred to a later phase that commits to overloads or codegen.

A4. `wf.run_until(handle)` raise contract — implement the method signature, raise `NotImplementedError` with a clear message if `handle.output_type is None`. Documented now to prevent downstream code from forming against partial behavior before MP-6 ungates it.

A5. Lint/doctor coverage of `str(handle)` coercion — extend `vibecomfy doctor` (search `vibecomfy/cli/`) to flag:
   - Raw string refs in block code where a typed Handle is in scope (warning).
   - Sites where `str(handle)` flows into `connect()` or dict keys (warning).
   New rule name per plan §"Lint / Guardrail Ideas": `untyped_raw_ref`.

A6. Parity tests for at least 3 ready templates: build via `wf.node(...).out(...)`, assert `compile("api")` matches `compile("graphbuilder")` (modulo allowed-difference set: node IDs may move, MarkdownNote may be stripped). Use `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` as one anchor.

### B. Custom-node lifecycle and lockfile enforcement (closes review §6, §7)

B1. `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` — new explicit verb. Refuses while a `run()` is in flight (see B2), closes existing `_comfy` context (mirror `stop()` at session.py:185-195 including the `model_mmap_residency` AttributeError swallow), reopens fresh `_comfy` (mirror `start()` at session.py:103-109), resets cached state carrying stale `NODE_CLASS_MAPPINGS` references. Implement equivalent for `ServerSession` (tear down and respawn the comfyui serve subprocess).

B2. **In-flight guard** on `session.stop()` and `reload_for_nodepack_change()` — today `EmbeddedSession.stop()` (session.py:185-195) doesn't check if a `run()` is awaiting `queue_prompt_api`. Add a tracking field (e.g. `_inflight_run: asyncio.Future | None`) and have stop() either refuse with a clear `RuntimeError`, OR cancel-and-await. Audit current `session.stop()` call sites; if both options reasonable, expose `wait_for_inflight: bool = True`.

B3. **`ServerSession` external-mode handling** — when the session targets an external-managed `comfyui serve` (one VibeComfy didn't spawn), `reload_for_nodepack_change()` MUST NOT terminate it. Raise `ExternalServerRestartRequired` (new error class) with a clear message. Today no detection path or error class exists — both must be created.

B4. **`vibecomfy nodes lock` CLI command** — write `custom_nodes.lock` after resolving any `semantic_label` values. Schema per plan §"Custom Nodes — Pinning, Hashes, and Lockfiles":
   - `git_commit_sha` (required primary pin — Git commit SHA-1, immutable).
   - `semantic_label` (optional advisory, e.g. "v1.4.0", "main").
   - `source_sha256` (optional per-node-class content digest, hex).
   Today's lockfile records `(name, sha, url)` only — see entries for `ComfyUI-KJNodes`, `ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper`. Extend format and migrate existing entries. Preserve `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` rename with backward-compat read.

B5. **`vibecomfy doctor` SHA verification verb** — read lockfile before `session.start()` (or as separate doctor invocation); verify each installed nodepack's git HEAD matches `git_commit_sha`. If `source_sha256` present, verify the named class file's content hash. Default on mismatch: **FAIL CLOSED**. Provide `--allow-drift` opt-in to downgrade to warning.

B6. **Tests**: in-flight guard, reload verb (with fake nodepack), external-server detection (assert `ExternalServerRestartRequired` raised, external process untouched), lockfile round-trip, SHA verification (synthetic mismatch fails closed without `--allow-drift`, warns with it).

### C. RunPod smoke-test scaffold (new)

C1. Add `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` (or `tests/runpod/`, follow existing test layout). The test:
   - Uses the `runpod_lifecycle` Python package (already available in this workspace; check `pip show runpod_lifecycle` or look in the broader workspace for the package). The package provisions ephemeral RunPod GPU pods, runs work, and tears them down.
   - Provisions one pod (cheap GPU class — e.g. RTX 4090 or A40), installs vibecomfy on it, runs ONE representative ready template through the new `wf.node(...).out(...)` typed-Handle authoring path, submits via `EmbeddedSession.run(...)` after a `reload_for_nodepack_change()` call (to exercise B1 + B2 paths), asserts the run produces expected outputs, tears the pod down.
   - Marked `@pytest.mark.runpod` so it's OPT-IN — never runs in default `pytest` invocations or CI without explicit opt-in.
   - Documented in the relevant test README or `docs/`: how to run it (`pytest -m runpod`), what credentials are required (RunPod API key env var), expected wall-clock cost, and the safety contract that it tears down on success AND failure.

C2. **DO NOT EXECUTE the smoke test as part of this megaplan run.** Provisioning real GPU pods burns credentials and budget — the megaplan executor must not do this. The deliverable is the test code + docs; the user (or post-merge CI) runs it explicitly.

### CRITICAL CONSTRAINTS

- **Backward compatibility is non-negotiable.** All existing tests must pass unchanged. Existing blocks returning `Handles({"image": "12.0"})` must keep working; tightening must coerce-with-warning. Existing `custom_nodes.lock` must still load (handle `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` migration). Existing `session.stop()` callers must keep working.
- Do NOT implement the compile-time serialization gate (MP-3 territory).
- Do NOT implement schema validation, `/object_info` snapshot, or `SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY` (MP-6 territory).
- Do NOT implement VibeFlow, ExternalPythonNode, `wrap_api_dict`, multi-stage orchestration, or stage-aware run-dir naming / `flow_id` / `stage_index` in `RunResult` (MP-5 territory; SD-013 in the plan).
- Do NOT expand the block library beyond what's needed for parity tests (MP-3 territory).
- Hot-loading a nodepack into a running session WITHOUT reload is explicitly out of scope per the plan.
- The RunPod smoke test must NOT be invoked during the megaplan execute phase.

### ACCEPTANCE

- All existing tests pass unchanged.
- New parity tests pass for 3+ ready templates.
- `Handle` is importable from a clean public path.
- `wf.node(...).out(...)` returns a Handle that compiles cleanly through `compile("api")`.
- `wf.run_until(handle)` raises `NotImplementedError` until `output_type` is populated.
- Doctor flags at least one synthetic `str(handle)` coercion case as `untyped_raw_ref` warning.
- New tests for in-flight guard, reload verb, external-server detection, lockfile round-trip, SHA verification all pass.
- `vibecomfy nodes lock` produces a lockfile in the new schema; existing entries migrate cleanly.
- `vibecomfy doctor` fails closed on synthetic SHA drift; succeeds with `--allow-drift`.
- `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` is exposed and documented.
- `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` (or equivalent) exists, is marked `@pytest.mark.runpod`, contains the smoke flow described in C1, and is documented for opt-in execution. The test is NOT executed as part of acceptance — only its existence and shape are verified.

User notes and answers:
- USER DECISION on B3 (ServerSession external-mode): adopt option (C) — DROP B3 entirely from this MP.

Rationale verified by reading code:
1. vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:12-14 already protects external servers — comfy_server(server_url=...) yields the URL without spawning, and the cleanup at lines 26-32 only fires when 'process' is non-None (i.e. only for managed subprocesses).
2. ServerSession in current production code ALWAYS owns its own subprocess (start() always calls _spawn_comfy_server). There is no production codepath that creates a ServerSession attached to an external URL — vibecomfy/runtime/run.py bypasses ServerSession entirely when given server_url=.
3. Therefore reload_for_nodepack_change() on ServerSession is always safe to tear down its own subprocess without external-mode branching, and adding _external + ExternalServerRestartRequired now would be unreachable mechanism (exactly what review §0 'every documented invariant needs a code-level check' said NOT to do).

Concrete plan changes for revise:
- REMOVE deliverable B3 entirely (no _external private flag, no ExternalServerRestartRequired class, no external-mode detection in ServerSession, no test for external-server-mode).
- Keep B1 (reload_for_nodepack_change), B2 (in-flight guard), B4 (vibecomfy nodes lock), B5 (doctor SHA verification), B6 (tests for B1/B2/B4/B5).
- Add ONE sentence in code comments / plan docs near the new reload_for_nodepack_change() noting: 'ServerSession external-mode (attach to a server VibeComfy didn't spawn) is deferred to MP-5 alongside session-shared multi-stage orchestration; current production paths use comfy_server(server_url=...) which already does the right thing.'
- All other deliverables (A1-A6, C1-C2) unchanged.

  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance and judgment calls:\n\nORDER MATTERS: T1 (Handle module) is the bedrock \u2014 every other phase-A task depends on it. Build T1 first and verify the import path works (`python -c \"from vibecomfy import Handle\"`) before touching anything else. Phase B is largely independent of Phase A and can be developed in parallel; T17 is the integration point.\n\nDOCTOR SEAM: The most subtle correctness item is the _read_doctor_lockfile() seam (T6) and the matching monkeypatch in T17 sub-step 6. The plan went through six iterations to converge on this design. Implement the seam as a top-level module function (not nested inside _cmd_doctor) so monkeypatch.setattr can target it. Recommend a shared autouse fixture in a tests/ conftest scoped to test_cli.py and test_doctor_models.py rather than per-test monkeypatch \u2014 cleaner and prevents drift if new doctor tests are added.\n\nBLOCK MIGRATION SWEEP: Run `grep -RIn 'Handles(' vibecomfy/blocks/` BEFORE editing T3, save the list, and check it off as you go. Easy to miss decode.py or video.py \u2014 both surfaced as iter-2 flag. The brief was explicit that the audit must be exhaustive.\n\nPARITY FIXTURES: Pre-pick anchors #2 and #3 before writing T7. flux2_klein_4b_t2i is required; suggest a Z-Image or SDXL variant + one audio/video template for structural variety. Document the choice in the test docstring so reviewers see the rationale.\n\nCONNECT SIGNATURE: For T4, vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py:connect (the helper) is called by block helpers, not user code. Preserve the legacy str + explicit output_slot kwarg branch; only the Handle-source branch is new. Don't break existing block helper callers.\n\nCONCURRENT RUN GUARD: T10's check-then-set sequence is racy if there's no lock, but Python asyncio's single-threaded event loop makes the check-and-set atomic between awaits. The guard is `if self._inflight_run is not None and not self._inflight_run.done(): raise RuntimeError(...)` followed immediately by `self._inflight_run = asyncio.current_task()` \u2014 no awaits between, so this is correct under asyncio semantics.\n\nRUNPOD CONFTEST: T18's pytest_collection_modifyitems must call BOTH `config.hook.pytest_deselected(items=deselected)` AND `items[:] = selected`. The hook reports deselection in pytest output ('=== N deselected ==='); the slice assignment is what actually removes them from the run. Skipping just the hook means tests still execute; skipping just the slice means deselection happens but isn't reported.\n\nDO NOT EXECUTE THE RUNPOD SMOKE TEST. The test scaffold's existence is the deliverable; running it provisions real GPU pods. T19's verdict is satisfied by file presence + structure, not by a green run.\n\nPR SHAPE: This will be a large diff \u2014 handles.py (new) + 8 blocks files + workflow.py + session.py + 2 commands + node_packs_lockfile.py (new) + ~10 test files + 2 docs. Tag the PR body with deliverable IDs A1\u2013A6, B1, B2, B4, B5, B6, C1\u2013C2 so reviewers can map sections to the brief.\n\nDEFERRED ITEMS (do not implement): MP-3 compile-time serialization gate, expanded block library; MP-5 VibeFlow, ExternalPythonNode, wrap_api_dict, flow_id/stage_index in RunResult, ServerSession external-mode handling; MP-6 object_info snapshot, SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY, named-output-to-slot resolution.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan places _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ but also needs vibeworkflow.connect in vibecomfy/workflow.py to consult handle_trace_active and record events. because importing vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log executes vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py, and that package currently imports vibeworkflow at top level, any top-level import from workflow.py to vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log would create a circular import risk unless the implementation uses carefully lazy imports or moves the shared trace module outside the blocks package. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure: does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the proposed _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ is supporting infrastructure that both blocks and workflow need. given the current package dependency direction (blocks imports workflow), the safer location is a neutral module such as vibecomfy/handles.py or vibecomfy/handle_warnings.py; leaving it under blocks increases import-order fragility unless the implementation is very deliberate. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them: find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: for the block helper caller path, vibeworkflow.connect is usually called by vibecomfy.blocks._utils.connect, not by the user's block function directly. the plan's immediate-caller-frame heuristic can work if _utils.connect has a handle local named source, but it will not identify the higher-level block function unless record_raw_ref_coercion preserves or walks to the block frame rather than skipping blocks frames. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] handle-equality-hash-semantics: strict python hash/eq invariant violated for the bare-id compatibility bridge (handle('1', 0) == '1' and == '1.0' but hash('1') != hash('1.0')). (flagged 2 times across 2 plans)
- [DEBT] handle-equality-hash-semantics: hash/eq compromise is documented but not strictly satisfied for bare-id bridge. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] import-architecture: import architecture: placing shared trace state in `vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log` risks circular imports because `workflow.py` needs the state for connect-site detection while `vibecomfy.blocks.__init__` currently imports `vibeworkflow` at package import time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: the plan's record_raw_ref_coercion helper says it finds the first non-vibecomfy/blocks frame for the site. that conflicts with the stated goal that raw string refs in block code are visible: a missed handles(image="...") inside a block called by a ready template could be attributed to an allowlisted ready_templates frame and suppressed, or at minimum would not identify the block module as the source. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] untyped-raw-ref-doctor-rule-coverage: runtime detection covers handles values and connect refs but not arbitrary python dict keys outside those flows. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] wf-node-out-api-contract: non-digit slot names raise notimplementederror instead of returning a handle; diverges from brief's .out(name_or_index) literal wording. (flagged 2 times across 2 plans)
- [DEBT] wf-node-out-api-contract: same as issue_hints-1. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)\n+\n+\n+def read_lockfile(path: Path = Path(\"custom_nodes.lock\")) -> list[LockEntry]:\n+    if not path.exists():\n+        return []\n+    text = path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n+    stripped = text.strip()\n+    if not stripped:\n+        return []\n+    if stripped[0] in {\"[\", \"=\"} or \"=\" in stripped.splitlines()[0]:\n+        return _read_toml_lockfile(stripped)\n+    return _read_legacy_lockfile(stripped)\n+\n+\n+def write_lockfile(entries: list[LockEntry], path: Path) -> None:\n+    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n+    lines = [\n+        \"# Generated by vibecomfy nodes lock.\",\n+        \"# git_commit_sha is the immutable primary pin; semantic_label is advisory.\",\n+        \"\",\n+    ]\n+    for entry in sorted(entries, key=lambda item: item.name):\n+        lines.append(f\"[nodepacks.{_toml_quote(entry.name)}]\")\n+        lines.append(f\"git_commit_sha = {_toml_quote(entry.git_commit_sha)}\")\n+        lines.append(f\"url = {_toml_quote(entry.url)}\")\n+        if entry.semantic_label is not None:\n+            lines.append(f\"semantic_label = {_toml_quote(entry.semantic_label)}\")\n+        if entry.source_sha256:\n+            lines.append(\"source_sha256 = {\")\n+            for name, digest in sorted(entry.source_sha256.items()):\n+                lines.append(f\"  {_toml_quote(name)} = {_toml_quote(digest)},\")\n+            lines.append(\"}\")\n+        lines.append(\"\")\n+    path.write_text(\"\\n\".join(lines), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n+\n+\n+def _read_legacy_lockfile(text: str) -> list[LockEntry]:\n+    entries: list[LockEntry] = []\n+    for lineno, line in enumerate(text.splitlines(), start=1):\n+        stripped = line.strip()\n+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith(\"#\"):\n+            continue\n+        parts = stripped.split()\n+        if len(parts) != 3:\n+            raise ValueError(f\"Invalid legacy custom_nodes.lock line {lineno}: expected 3 whitespace tokens\")\n+        name, git_commit_sha, url = parts\n+        entries.append(LockEntry(name=name, git_commit_sha=git_commit_sha, url=url))\n+    return entries\n+\n+\n+def _read_toml_lockfile(text: str) -> list[LockEntry]:\n+    data = tomllib.loads(text)\n+    entries: list[LockEntry] = []\n+    if isinstance(data.get(\"entries\"), list):\n+        for raw in data[\"entries\"]:\n+            if isinstance(raw, dict):\n+                entries.append(_entry_from_mapping(raw.get(\"name\"), raw))\n+        return entries\n+    nodepacks = data.get(\"nodepacks\")\n+    if isinstance(nodepacks, dict):\n+        for name, raw in nodepacks.items():\n+            if isinstance(raw, dict):\n+                entries.append(_entry_from_mapping(name, raw))\n+        return entries\n+    for name, raw in data.items():\n+        if isinstance(raw, dict) and (\"git_commit_sha\" in raw or \"git_sha\" in raw):\n+            entries.append(_entry_from_mapping(name, raw))\n+    return entries\n+\n+\n+def _entry_from_mapping(name: Any, raw: dict[str, Any]) -> LockEntry:\n+    entry_name = str(raw.get(\"name\") or name)\n+    git_commit_sha = raw.get(\"git_commit_sha\", raw.get(\"git_sha\"))\n+    if git_commit_sha is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Lock entry {entry_name!r} is missing git_commit_sha\")\n+    url = raw.get(\"url\")\n+    if url is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Lock entry {entry_name!r} is missing url\")\n+    source_sha256 = raw.get(\"source_sha256\") or {}\n+    if not isinstance(source_sha256, dict):\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Lock entry {entry_name!r} source_sha256 must be a table\")\n+    semantic_label = raw.get(\"semantic_label\")\n+    return LockEntry(\n+        name=entry_name,\n+        git_commit_sha=str(git_commit_sha),\n+        url=str(url),\n+        semantic_label=str(semantic_label) if semantic_label is not None else None,\n+        source_sha256={str(key): str(value) for key, value in source_sha256.items()},\n+    )\n+\n+\n+def _toml_quote(value: str) -> str:\n+    escaped = value.replace(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\").replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n+    return f'\"{escaped}\"'\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

        Idea:
P1 Foundation: typed Handle scaffold + custom-node lifecycle/lockfile + RunPod smoke-test scaffold.

This is the combined first phase of implementing the Python composition DSL designed in `docs/python_composition_dsl_plan.md` (recently revised — read the current version) and addressing the issues called out in `docs/python_composition_dsl_review.md`. It folds together two originally-separate efforts (MP-2 typed Handles, MP-4 lifecycle/lockfile) plus a RunPod-based smoke-test scaffold so the whole thing can ship as one squashable PR.

Two later phases will build on this output: (a) MP-3 will expand the block library and add the compile-time serialization gate; (b) MP-5 will introduce VibeFlow multi-stage orchestration. Do NOT preempt their scope.

DELIVERABLES — all must land:

### A. Typed Handle scaffold (closes review §1, §2, §3, §10)

A1. `Handle` dataclass — frozen, fields: `node_id` (str), `output_slot` (str | int), `output_type` (str | None — populated later by MP-6 schema integration), `name` (str | None).
   - `__str__` returns `f"{node_id}.{output_slot}"` matching the existing string-ref convention (see `vibecomfy/blocks/subgraph.py:47` precedent and `vibecomfy/workflow.py:128-132` consumer that splits on `.`).
   - `__eq__` matches BOTH another Handle (by node_id+slot) AND its string form, so `handle == "12.0"` works without manual `str()`. Closes the silent type-erasure hole.
   - `__hash__` consistent with `__eq__`.

A2. Tighten `Handles` (plural wrapper at `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:11-35`) — change value type from `Mapping[str, Any]` to `Mapping[str, Handle]`. Update construction sites in `vibecomfy/blocks/` (notably `subgraph.py`) to wrap string refs as Handle instances. Backward-compat: callers passing raw strings must be coerced with a `DeprecationWarning` once per call site.

A3. `wf.node(class_type, **kwargs)` — typed authoring wrapper over `VibeWorkflow.add_node(...)` (`vibecomfy/workflow.py:122-126`). Returns an object with `.out(name_or_index) -> Handle` and `.id` (matches GraphBuilder's `id=` convention). Per the revised plan's API Concepts, this is the canonical authoring entry point. Does NOT promise mypy-grade safety; `T` parameterization deferred to a later phase that commits to overloads or codegen.

A4. `wf.run_until(handle)` raise contract — implement the method signature, raise `NotImplementedError` with a clear message if `handle.output_type is None`. Documented now to prevent downstream code from forming against partial behavior before MP-6 ungates it.

A5. Lint/doctor coverage of `str(handle)` coercion — extend `vibecomfy doctor` (search `vibecomfy/cli/`) to flag:
   - Raw string refs in block code where a typed Handle is in scope (warning).
   - Sites where `str(handle)` flows into `connect()` or dict keys (warning).
   New rule name per plan §"Lint / Guardrail Ideas": `untyped_raw_ref`.

A6. Parity tests for at least 3 ready templates: build via `wf.node(...).out(...)`, assert `compile("api")` matches `compile("graphbuilder")` (modulo allowed-difference set: node IDs may move, MarkdownNote may be stripped). Use `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` as one anchor.

### B. Custom-node lifecycle and lockfile enforcement (closes review §6, §7)

B1. `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` — new explicit verb. Refuses while a `run()` is in flight (see B2), closes existing `_comfy` context (mirror `stop()` at session.py:185-195 including the `model_mmap_residency` AttributeError swallow), reopens fresh `_comfy` (mirror `start()` at session.py:103-109), resets cached state carrying stale `NODE_CLASS_MAPPINGS` references. Implement equivalent for `ServerSession` (tear down and respawn the comfyui serve subprocess).

B2. **In-flight guard** on `session.stop()` and `reload_for_nodepack_change()` — today `EmbeddedSession.stop()` (session.py:185-195) doesn't check if a `run()` is awaiting `queue_prompt_api`. Add a tracking field (e.g. `_inflight_run: asyncio.Future | None`) and have stop() either refuse with a clear `RuntimeError`, OR cancel-and-await. Audit current `session.stop()` call sites; if both options reasonable, expose `wait_for_inflight: bool = True`.

B3. **`ServerSession` external-mode handling** — when the session targets an external-managed `comfyui serve` (one VibeComfy didn't spawn), `reload_for_nodepack_change()` MUST NOT terminate it. Raise `ExternalServerRestartRequired` (new error class) with a clear message. Today no detection path or error class exists — both must be created.

B4. **`vibecomfy nodes lock` CLI command** — write `custom_nodes.lock` after resolving any `semantic_label` values. Schema per plan §"Custom Nodes — Pinning, Hashes, and Lockfiles":
   - `git_commit_sha` (required primary pin — Git commit SHA-1, immutable).
   - `semantic_label` (optional advisory, e.g. "v1.4.0", "main").
   - `source_sha256` (optional per-node-class content digest, hex).
   Today's lockfile records `(name, sha, url)` only — see entries for `ComfyUI-KJNodes`, `ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper`. Extend format and migrate existing entries. Preserve `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` rename with backward-compat read.

B5. **`vibecomfy doctor` SHA verification verb** — read lockfile before `session.start()` (or as separate doctor invocation); verify each installed nodepack's git HEAD matches `git_commit_sha`. If `source_sha256` present, verify the named class file's content hash. Default on mismatch: **FAIL CLOSED**. Provide `--allow-drift` opt-in to downgrade to warning.

B6. **Tests**: in-flight guard, reload verb (with fake nodepack), external-server detection (assert `ExternalServerRestartRequired` raised, external process untouched), lockfile round-trip, SHA verification (synthetic mismatch fails closed without `--allow-drift`, warns with it).

### C. RunPod smoke-test scaffold (new)

C1. Add `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` (or `tests/runpod/`, follow existing test layout). The test:
   - Uses the `runpod_lifecycle` Python package (already available in this workspace; check `pip show runpod_lifecycle` or look in the broader workspace for the package). The package provisions ephemeral RunPod GPU pods, runs work, and tears them down.
   - Provisions one pod (cheap GPU class — e.g. RTX 4090 or A40), installs vibecomfy on it, runs ONE representative ready template through the new `wf.node(...).out(...)` typed-Handle authoring path, submits via `EmbeddedSession.run(...)` after a `reload_for_nodepack_change()` call (to exercise B1 + B2 paths), asserts the run produces expected outputs, tears the pod down.
   - Marked `@pytest.mark.runpod` so it's OPT-IN — never runs in default `pytest` invocations or CI without explicit opt-in.
   - Documented in the relevant test README or `docs/`: how to run it (`pytest -m runpod`), what credentials are required (RunPod API key env var), expected wall-clock cost, and the safety contract that it tears down on success AND failure.

C2. **DO NOT EXECUTE the smoke test as part of this megaplan run.** Provisioning real GPU pods burns credentials and budget — the megaplan executor must not do this. The deliverable is the test code + docs; the user (or post-merge CI) runs it explicitly.

### CRITICAL CONSTRAINTS

- **Backward compatibility is non-negotiable.** All existing tests must pass unchanged. Existing blocks returning `Handles({"image": "12.0"})` must keep working; tightening must coerce-with-warning. Existing `custom_nodes.lock` must still load (handle `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` migration). Existing `session.stop()` callers must keep working.
- Do NOT implement the compile-time serialization gate (MP-3 territory).
- Do NOT implement schema validation, `/object_info` snapshot, or `SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY` (MP-6 territory).
- Do NOT implement VibeFlow, ExternalPythonNode, `wrap_api_dict`, multi-stage orchestration, or stage-aware run-dir naming / `flow_id` / `stage_index` in `RunResult` (MP-5 territory; SD-013 in the plan).
- Do NOT expand the block library beyond what's needed for parity tests (MP-3 territory).
- Hot-loading a nodepack into a running session WITHOUT reload is explicitly out of scope per the plan.
- The RunPod smoke test must NOT be invoked during the megaplan execute phase.

### KEY FILES (read first, before planning)

- `docs/python_composition_dsl_plan.md` — full design (recently revised: includes new "Desired Authoring Experience" north-star section, API Concepts collapse, SD-013 lifecycle settlement, ExternalPythonNode marked Aspirational/P3, "Enforcement Gates" section, "End-to-End Implementation Guidance" section).
- `docs/python_composition_dsl_review.md` — issues this MP closes.
- `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py` — current `Handles` + `Block` protocol.
- `vibecomfy/blocks/subgraph.py` — string-form handle precedent at line 47.
- `vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py` — existing provenance pattern (preserve it).
- `vibecomfy/workflow.py` — `VibeWorkflow` IR, `add_node` (122-126), `connect` (128-132), `compile` (176-188), graphbuilder backend.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py` — especially:
  - 88-195: `EmbeddedSession`
  - 198-299: `ServerSession`
  - 144: `queue_prompt_api` (currently unshielded)
  - 185-195: `EmbeddedSession.stop()` (no in-flight check)
  - 281-290: `ServerSession.reconfigure` (only argv changes)
  - 487-507: `_run_metadata`
- `vibecomfy/cli/` (search for `vibecomfy nodes` and `vibecomfy doctor` command modules).
- `custom_nodes.lock` — current entries.
- `docs/custom_nodes.md` — existing custom-node docs.
- `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` — parity test anchor.
- For RunPod scaffolding: locate the `runpod_lifecycle` package (try `pip show runpod_lifecycle`, search `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/` for the package source, or check the workspace's `pyproject.toml` for dependency reference). Read its README for the provisioning API.

### ACCEPTANCE

- All existing tests pass unchanged.
- New parity tests pass for 3+ ready templates.
- `Handle` is importable from a clean public path.
- `wf.node(...).out(...)` returns a Handle that compiles cleanly through `compile("api")`.
- `wf.run_until(handle)` raises `NotImplementedError` until `output_type` is populated.
- Doctor flags at least one synthetic `str(handle)` coercion case as `untyped_raw_ref` warning.
- New tests for in-flight guard, reload verb, external-server detection, lockfile round-trip, SHA verification all pass.
- `vibecomfy nodes lock` produces a lockfile in the new schema; existing entries migrate cleanly.
- `vibecomfy doctor` fails closed on synthetic SHA drift; succeeds with `--allow-drift`.
- `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` is exposed and documented.
- `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` (or equivalent) exists, is marked `@pytest.mark.runpod`, contains the smoke flow described in C1, and is documented for opt-in execution. The test is NOT executed as part of acceptance — only its existence and shape are verified.

User notes and answers:
- USER DECISION on B3 (ServerSession external-mode): adopt option (C) — DROP B3 entirely from this MP.

Rationale verified by reading code:
1. vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:12-14 already protects external servers — comfy_server(server_url=...) yields the URL without spawning, and the cleanup at lines 26-32 only fires when 'process' is non-None (i.e. only for managed subprocesses).
2. ServerSession in current production code ALWAYS owns its own subprocess (start() always calls _spawn_comfy_server). There is no production codepath that creates a ServerSession attached to an external URL — vibecomfy/runtime/run.py bypasses ServerSession entirely when given server_url=.
3. Therefore reload_for_nodepack_change() on ServerSession is always safe to tear down its own subprocess without external-mode branching, and adding _external + ExternalServerRestartRequired now would be unreachable mechanism (exactly what review §0 'every documented invariant needs a code-level check' said NOT to do).

Concrete plan changes for revise:
- REMOVE deliverable B3 entirely (no _external private flag, no ExternalServerRestartRequired class, no external-mode detection in ServerSession, no test for external-server-mode).

      "description": "Add in-flight task tracking + concurrent-run guard + stop(wait_for_inflight) to vibecomfy/runtime/session.py. In both EmbeddedSession.__init__ and ServerSession.__init__: add `self._inflight_run: asyncio.Task | None = None`. At the top of each class's `run()` method, before any other work: if self._inflight_run is not None and not self._inflight_run.done() \u2192 raise RuntimeError('session already has a run in flight; concurrent run() is not supported in P1'); then `self._inflight_run = asyncio.current_task()`. In a finally block at the end of run(): `self._inflight_run = None`. Wrap the entire body of run() so the tracker covers queue submit + watchdog finalization + metadata write. Modify EmbeddedSession.stop and ServerSession.stop to take `wait_for_inflight: bool = True`: if _inflight_run set + done \u2192 clear field, proceed with normal teardown; if set + not done + wait_for_inflight=True \u2192 await self._inflight_run, catch and re-raise the run's own exception before tearing down; if set + not done + wait_for_inflight=False \u2192 raise RuntimeError('session.stop() called while a run is in flight; pass wait_for_inflight=True or call after run completes'); no _inflight_run \u2192 existing behavior unchanged so current tests/test_runtime_session.py callers stay green.",

  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance and judgment calls:\n\nORDER MATTERS: T1 (Handle module) is the bedrock \u2014 every other phase-A task depends on it. Build T1 first and verify the import path works (`python -c \"from vibecomfy import Handle\"`) before touching anything else. Phase B is largely independent of Phase A and can be developed in parallel; T17 is the integration point.\n\nDOCTOR SEAM: The most subtle correctness item is the _read_doctor_lockfile() seam (T6) and the matching monkeypatch in T17 sub-step 6. The plan went through six iterations to converge on this design. Implement the seam as a top-level module function (not nested inside _cmd_doctor) so monkeypatch.setattr can target it. Recommend a shared autouse fixture in a tests/ conftest scoped to test_cli.py and test_doctor_models.py rather than per-test monkeypatch \u2014 cleaner and prevents drift if new doctor tests are added.\n\nBLOCK MIGRATION SWEEP: Run `grep -RIn 'Handles(' vibecomfy/blocks/` BEFORE editing T3, save the list, and check it off as you go. Easy to miss decode.py or video.py \u2014 both surfaced as iter-2 flag. The brief was explicit that the audit must be exhaustive.\n\nPARITY FIXTURES: Pre-pick anchors #2 and #3 before writing T7. flux2_klein_4b_t2i is required; suggest a Z-Image or SDXL variant + one audio/video template for structural variety. Document the choice in the test docstring so reviewers see the rationale.\n\nCONNECT SIGNATURE: For T4, vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py:connect (the helper) is called by block helpers, not user code. Preserve the legacy str + explicit output_slot kwarg branch; only the Handle-source branch is new. Don't break existing block helper callers.\n\nCONCURRENT RUN GUARD: T10's check-then-set sequence is racy if there's no lock, but Python asyncio's single-threaded event loop makes the check-and-set atomic between awaits. The guard is `if self._inflight_run is not None and not self._inflight_run.done(): raise RuntimeError(...)` followed immediately by `self._inflight_run = asyncio.current_task()` \u2014 no awaits between, so this is correct under asyncio semantics.\n\nRUNPOD CONFTEST: T18's pytest_collection_modifyitems must call BOTH `config.hook.pytest_deselected(items=deselected)` AND `items[:] = selected`. The hook reports deselection in pytest output ('=== N deselected ==='); the slice assignment is what actually removes them from the run. Skipping just the hook means tests still execute; skipping just the slice means deselection happens but isn't reported.\n\nDO NOT EXECUTE THE RUNPOD SMOKE TEST. The test scaffold's existence is the deliverable; running it provisions real GPU pods. T19's verdict is satisfied by file presence + structure, not by a green run.\n\nPR SHAPE: This will be a large diff \u2014 handles.py (new) + 8 blocks files + workflow.py + session.py + 2 commands + node_packs_lockfile.py (new) + ~10 test files + 2 docs. Tag the PR body with deliverable IDs A1\u2013A6, B1, B2, B4, B5, B6, C1\u2013C2 so reviewers can map sections to the brief.\n\nDEFERRED ITEMS (do not implement): MP-3 compile-time serialization gate, expanded block library; MP-5 VibeFlow, ExternalPythonNode, wrap_api_dict, flow_id/stage_index in RunResult, ServerSession external-mode handling; MP-6 object_info snapshot, SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY, named-output-to-slot resolution.",

New authoring code should prefer `wf.node(...).out(<int_slot>)`, which returns a `Handle` carrying the source node id, output slot, optional output type, and optional name. Blocks should return `Handles({\"image\": Handle(node_id=node.id, output_slot=0, name=\"image\")})` instead of raw string references.\n+\n+Named output strings such as `.out(\"IMAGE\")` are intentionally gated until MP-6 schema integration can map Comfy output names to slots. In P1, pass an integer slot or digit string; non-numeric output names raise `NotImplementedError`.\n*** End Patch\n"
}

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

        Idea:
P1 Foundation: typed Handle scaffold + custom-node lifecycle/lockfile + RunPod smoke-test scaffold.

This is the combined first phase of implementing the Python composition DSL designed in `docs/python_composition_dsl_plan.md` (recently revised — read the current version) and addressing the issues called out in `docs/python_composition_dsl_review.md`. It folds together two originally-separate efforts (MP-2 typed Handles, MP-4 lifecycle/lockfile) plus a RunPod-based smoke-test scaffold so the whole thing can ship as one squashable PR.

Two later phases will build on this output: (a) MP-3 will expand the block library and add the compile-time serialization gate; (b) MP-5 will introduce VibeFlow multi-stage orchestration. Do NOT preempt their scope.

DELIVERABLES — all must land:

### A. Typed Handle scaffold (closes review §1, §2, §3, §10)

A1. `Handle` dataclass — frozen, fields: `node_id` (str), `output_slot` (str | int), `output_type` (str | None — populated later by MP-6 schema integration), `name` (str | None).
   - `__str__` returns `f"{node_id}.{output_slot}"` matching the existing string-ref convention (see `vibecomfy/blocks/subgraph.py:47` precedent and `vibecomfy/workflow.py:128-132` consumer that splits on `.`).
   - `__eq__` matches BOTH another Handle (by node_id+slot) AND its string form, so `handle == "12.0"` works without manual `str()`. Closes the silent type-erasure hole.
   - `__hash__` consistent with `__eq__`.

A2. Tighten `Handles` (plural wrapper at `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:11-35`) — change value type from `Mapping[str, Any]` to `Mapping[str, Handle]`. Update construction sites in `vibecomfy/blocks/` (notably `subgraph.py`) to wrap string refs as Handle instances. Backward-compat: callers passing raw strings must be coerced with a `DeprecationWarning` once per call site.

A3. `wf.node(class_type, **kwargs)` — typed authoring wrapper over `VibeWorkflow.add_node(...)` (`vibecomfy/workflow.py:122-126`). Returns an object with `.out(name_or_index) -> Handle` and `.id` (matches GraphBuilder's `id=` convention). Per the revised plan's API Concepts, this is the canonical authoring entry point. Does NOT promise mypy-grade safety; `T` parameterization deferred to a later phase that commits to overloads or codegen.

A4. `wf.run_until(handle)` raise contract — implement the method signature, raise `NotImplementedError` with a clear message if `handle.output_type is None`. Documented now to prevent downstream code from forming against partial behavior before MP-6 ungates it.

A5. Lint/doctor coverage of `str(handle)` coercion — extend `vibecomfy doctor` (search `vibecomfy/cli/`) to flag:
   - Raw string refs in block code where a typed Handle is in scope (warning).
   - Sites where `str(handle)` flows into `connect()` or dict keys (warning).
   New rule name per plan §"Lint / Guardrail Ideas": `untyped_raw_ref`.

A6. Parity tests for at least 3 ready templates: build via `wf.node(...).out(...)`, assert `compile("api")` matches `compile("graphbuilder")` (modulo allowed-difference set: node IDs may move, MarkdownNote may be stripped). Use `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` as one anchor.

### B. Custom-node lifecycle and lockfile enforcement (closes review §6, §7)

B1. `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` — new explicit verb. Refuses while a `run()` is in flight (see B2), closes existing `_comfy` context (mirror `stop()` at session.py:185-195 including the `model_mmap_residency` AttributeError swallow), reopens fresh `_comfy` (mirror `start()` at session.py:103-109), resets cached state carrying stale `NODE_CLASS_MAPPINGS` references. Implement equivalent for `ServerSession` (tear down and respawn the comfyui serve subprocess).

B2. **In-flight guard** on `session.stop()` and `reload_for_nodepack_change()` — today `EmbeddedSession.stop()` (session.py:185-195) doesn't check if a `run()` is awaiting `queue_prompt_api`. Add a tracking field (e.g. `_inflight_run: asyncio.Future | None`) and have stop() either refuse with a clear `RuntimeError`, OR cancel-and-await. Audit current `session.stop()` call sites; if both options reasonable, expose `wait_for_inflight: bool = True`.

B3. **`ServerSession` external-mode handling** — when the session targets an external-managed `comfyui serve` (one VibeComfy didn't spawn), `reload_for_nodepack_change()` MUST NOT terminate it. Raise `ExternalServerRestartRequired` (new error class) with a clear message. Today no detection path or error class exists — both must be created.

B4. **`vibecomfy nodes lock` CLI command** — write `custom_nodes.lock` after resolving any `semantic_label` values. Schema per plan §"Custom Nodes — Pinning, Hashes, and Lockfiles":
   - `git_commit_sha` (required primary pin — Git commit SHA-1, immutable).
   - `semantic_label` (optional advisory, e.g. "v1.4.0", "main").
   - `source_sha256` (optional per-node-class content digest, hex).
   Today's lockfile records `(name, sha, url)` only — see entries for `ComfyUI-KJNodes`, `ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper`. Extend format and migrate existing entries. Preserve `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` rename with backward-compat read.

B5. **`vibecomfy doctor` SHA verification verb** — read lockfile before `session.start()` (or as separate doctor invocation); verify each installed nodepack's git HEAD matches `git_commit_sha`. If `source_sha256` present, verify the named class file's content hash. Default on mismatch: **FAIL CLOSED**. Provide `--allow-drift` opt-in to downgrade to warning.

B6. **Tests**: in-flight guard, reload verb (with fake nodepack), external-server detection (assert `ExternalServerRestartRequired` raised, external process untouched), lockfile round-trip, SHA verification (synthetic mismatch fails closed without `--allow-drift`, warns with it).

### C. RunPod smoke-test scaffold (new)

C1. Add `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` (or `tests/runpod/`, follow existing test layout). The test:
   - Uses the `runpod_lifecycle` Python package (already available in this workspace; check `pip show runpod_lifecycle` or look in the broader workspace for the package). The package provisions ephemeral RunPod GPU pods, runs work, and tears them down.
   - Provisions one pod (cheap GPU class — e.g. RTX 4090 or A40), installs vibecomfy on it, runs ONE representative ready template through the new `wf.node(...).out(...)` typed-Handle authoring path, submits via `EmbeddedSession.run(...)` after a `reload_for_nodepack_change()` call (to exercise B1 + B2 paths), asserts the run produces expected outputs, tears the pod down.
   - Marked `@pytest.mark.runpod` so it's OPT-IN — never runs in default `pytest` invocations or CI without explicit opt-in.
   - Documented in the relevant test README or `docs/`: how to run it (`pytest -m runpod`), what credentials are required (RunPod API key env var), expected wall-clock cost, and the safety contract that it tears down on success AND failure.

C2. **DO NOT EXECUTE the smoke test as part of this megaplan run.** Provisioning real GPU pods burns credentials and budget — the megaplan executor must not do this. The deliverable is the test code + docs; the user (or post-merge CI) runs it explicitly.

### CRITICAL CONSTRAINTS

- **Backward compatibility is non-negotiable.** All existing tests must pass unchanged. Existing blocks returning `Handles({"image": "12.0"})` must keep working; tightening must coerce-with-warning. Existing `custom_nodes.lock` must still load (handle `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` migration). Existing `session.stop()` callers must keep working.

- Do NOT expand the block library beyond what's needed for parity tests (MP-3 territory).
- Hot-loading a nodepack into a running session WITHOUT reload is explicitly out of scope per the plan.
- The RunPod smoke test must NOT be invoked during the megaplan execute phase.

### ACCEPTANCE

User notes and answers:
- USER DECISION on B3 (ServerSession external-mode): adopt option (C) — DROP B3 entirely from this MP.

Rationale verified by reading code:
1. vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:12-14 already protects external servers — comfy_server(server_url=...) yields the URL without spawning, and the cleanup at lines 26-32 only fires when 'process' is non-None (i.e. only for managed subprocesses).
2. ServerSession in current production code ALWAYS owns its own subprocess (start() always calls _spawn_comfy_server). There is no production codepath that creates a ServerSession attached to an external URL — vibecomfy/runtime/run.py bypasses ServerSession entirely when given server_url=.
3. Therefore reload_for_nodepack_change() on ServerSession is always safe to tear down its own subprocess without external-mode branching, and adding _external + ExternalServerRestartRequired now would be unreachable mechanism (exactly what review §0 'every documented invariant needs a code-level check' said NOT to do).

Concrete plan changes for revise:
- REMOVE deliverable B3 entirely (no _external private flag, no ExternalServerRestartRequired class, no external-mode detection in ServerSession, no test for external-server-mode).
- Keep B1 (reload_for_nodepack_change), B2 (in-flight guard), B4 (vibecomfy nodes lock), B5 (doctor SHA verification), B6 (tests for B1/B2/B4/B5).
- Add ONE sentence in code comments / plan docs near the new reload_for_nodepack_change() noting: 'ServerSession external-mode (attach to a server VibeComfy didn't spawn) is deferred to MP-5 alongside session-shared multi-stage orchestration; current production paths use comfy_server(server_url=...) which already does the right thing.'
- All other deliverables (A1-A6, C1-C2) unchanged.

After revising, run critique → gate. The 6 remaining flags should collapse since they all clustered on B3.

      "id": "T10",
      "description": "Add in-flight task tracking + concurrent-run guard + stop(wait_for_inflight) to vibecomfy/runtime/session.py. In both EmbeddedSession.__init__ and ServerSession.__init__: add `self._inflight_run: asyncio.Task | None = None`. At the top of each class's `run()` method, before any other work: if self._inflight_run is not None and not self._inflight_run.done() \u2192 raise RuntimeError('session already has a run in flight; concurrent run() is not supported in P1'); then `self._inflight_run = asyncio.current_task()`. In a finally block at the end of run(): `self._inflight_run = None`. Wrap the entire body of run() so the tracker covers queue submit + watchdog finalization + metadata write. Modify EmbeddedSession.stop and ServerSession.stop to take `wait_for_inflight: bool = True`: if _inflight_run set + done \u2192 clear field, proceed with normal teardown; if set + not done + wait_for_inflight=True \u2192 await self._inflight_run, catch and re-raise the run's own exception before tearing down; if set + not done + wait_for_inflight=False \u2192 raise RuntimeError('session.stop() called while a run is in flight; pass wait_for_inflight=True or call after run completes'); no _inflight_run \u2192 existing behavior unchanged so current tests/test_runtime_session.py callers stay green.",
      "depends_on": [],
      "status": "done",
      "executor_notes": "Added _inflight_run fields to EmbeddedSession and ServerSession, wrapped run() with check-then-set concurrent guard, and moved existing full run body into _run_untracked so tracking covers queueing, watchdog finalization, metadata, and return. stop(wait_for_inflight=True) now awaits the in-flight task and re-raises exceptions before teardown; wait_for_inflight=False raises the required RuntimeError.   "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance and judgment calls:\n\nORDER MATTERS: T1 (Handle module) is the bedrock \u2014 every other phase-A task depends on it. Build T1 first and verify the import path works (`python -c \"from vibecomfy import Handle\"`) before touching anything else. Phase B is largely independent of Phase A and can be developed in parallel; T17 is the integration point.\n\nDOCTOR SEAM: The most subtle correctness item is the _read_doctor_lockfile() seam (T6) and the matching monkeypatch in T17 sub-step 6. The plan went through six iterations to converge on this design. Implement the seam as a top-level module function (not nested inside _cmd_doctor) so monkeypatch.setattr can target it. Recommend a shared autouse fixture in a tests/ conftest scoped to test_cli.py and test_doctor_models.py rather than per-test monkeypatch \u2014 cleaner and prevents drift if new doctor tests are added.\n\nBLOCK MIGRATION SWEEP: Run `grep -RIn 'Handles(' vibecomfy/blocks/` BEFORE editing T3, save the list, and check it off as you go. Easy to miss decode.py or video.py \u2014 both surfaced as iter-2 flag. The brief was explicit that the audit must be exhaustive.\n\nPARITY FIXTURES: Pre-pick anchors #2 and #3 before writing T7. flux2_klein_4b_t2i is required; suggest a Z-Image or SDXL variant + one audio/video template for structural variety. Document the choice in the test docstring so reviewers see the rationale.\n\nCONNECT SIGNATURE: For T4, vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py:connect (the helper) is called by block helpers, not user code. Preserve the legacy str + explicit output_slot kwarg branch; only the Handle-source branch is new. Don't break existing block helper callers.\n\nCONCURRENT RUN GUARD: T10's check-then-set sequence is racy if there's no lock, but Python asyncio's single-threaded event loop makes the check-and-set atomic between awaits. The guard is `if self._inflight_run is not None and not self._inflight_run.done(): raise RuntimeError(...)` followed immediately by `self._inflight_run = asyncio.current_task()` \u2014 no awaits between, so this is correct under asyncio semantics.\n\nRUNPOD CONFTEST: T18's pytest_collection_modifyitems must call BOTH `config.hook.pytest_deselected(items=deselected)` AND `items[:] = selected`. The hook reports deselection in pytest output ('=== N deselected ==='); the slice assignment is what actually removes them from the run. Skipping just the hook means tests still execute; skipping just the slice means deselection happens but isn't reported.\n\nDO NOT EXECUTE THE RUNPOD SMOKE TEST. The test scaffold's existence is the deliverable; running it provisions real GPU pods. T19's verdict is satisfied by file presence + structure, not by a green run.\n\nPR SHAPE: This will be a large diff \u2014 handles.py (new) + 8 blocks files + workflow.py + session.py + 2 commands + node_packs_lockfile.py (new) + ~10 test files + 2 docs. Tag the PR body with deliverable IDs A1\u2013A6, B1, B2, B4, B5, B6, C1\u2013C2 so reviewers can map sections to the brief.\n\nDEFERRED ITEMS (do not implement): MP-3 compile-time serialization gate, expanded block library; MP-5 VibeFlow, ExternalPythonNode, wrap_api_dict, flow_id/stage_index in RunResult, ServerSession external-mode handling; MP-6 object_info snapshot, SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY, named-output-to-slot resolution.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan places _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ but also needs vibeworkflow.connect in vibecomfy/workflow.py to consult handle_trace_active and record events. because importing vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log executes vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py, and that package currently imports vibeworkflow at top level, any top-level import from workflow.py to vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log would create a circular import risk unless the implementation uses carefully lazy imports or moves the shared trace module outside the blocks package. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure: does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the proposed _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ is supporting infrastructure that both blocks and workflow need. given the current package dependency direction (blocks imports workflow), the safer location is a neutral module such as vibecomfy/handles.py or vibecomfy/handle_warnings.py; leaving it under blocks increases import-order fragility unless the implementation is very deliberate. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them: find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: for the block helper caller path, vibeworkflow.connect is usually called by vibecomfy.blocks._utils.connect, not by the user's block function directly. the plan's immediate-caller-frame heuristic can work if _utils.connect has a handle local named source, but it will not identify the higher-level block function unless record_raw_ref_coercion preserves or walks to the block frame rather than skipping blocks frames. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] handle-equality-hash-semantics: strict python hash/eq invariant violated for the bare-id compatibility bridge (handle('1', 0) == '1' and == '1.0' but hash('1') != hash('1.0')). (flagged 2 times across 2 plans)
- [DEBT] handle-equality-hash-semantics: hash/eq compromise is documented but not strictly satisfied for bare-id bridge. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] import-architecture: import architecture: placing shared trace state in `vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log` risks circular imports because `workflow.py` needs the state for connect-site detection while `vibecomfy.blocks.__init__` currently imports `vibeworkflow` at package import time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] search-for-related-code-that-handles-the-same-concept-is-the-reported-issue-a-symptom-of-something-broader: search for related code that handles the same concept. is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: the plan's record_raw_ref_coercion helper says it finds the first non-vibecomfy/blocks frame for the site. that conflicts with the stated goal that raw string refs in block code are visible: a missed handles(image="...") inside a block called by a ready template could be attributed to an allowlisted ready_templates frame and suppressed, or at minimum would not identify the block module as the source. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] untyped-raw-ref-doctor-rule-coverage: runtime detection covers handles values and connect refs but not arbitrary python dict keys outside those flows. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] wf-node-out-api-contract: non-digit slot names raise notimplementederror instead of returning a handle; diverges from brief's .out(name_or_index) literal wording. (flagged 2 times across 2 plans)
- [DEBT] wf-node-out-api-contract: same as issue_hints-1. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy/vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py\n@@\n import ast\n+import hashlib\n import json\n import os\n from pathlib import Path\n import re\n+import subprocess\n from typing import Any\n@@\n def _cmd_doctor(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:\n     lint = getattr(args, \"lint\", False)\n-    _allow_drift = getattr(args, \"allow_drift\", False)\n+    allow_drift = getattr(args, \"allow_drift\", False)\n     schema_provider = get_schema_provider(\"auto\")\n     try:\n         workflow = load_workflow_reference(args.path, schema_provider=schema_provider, allow_scratchpad=True)\n@@\n     if lint:\n         for warning in _lint_untyped_raw_refs(Path(args.path)):\n             print(f\"- untyped_raw_ref: {warning}\")\n+    drift_warnings, drift_errors = _nodepack_lockfile_drift()\n+    if drift_errors:\n+        if allow_drift:\n+            print(\"Nodepack lockfile drift warnings:\")\n+            for warning in [*drift_warnings, *drift_errors]:\n+                print(f\"- {warning}\")\n+            return 0\n+        print(\"Layer: nodepack lockfile drift\")\n+        for error in drift_errors:\n+            print(f\"- {error}\")\n+        return 1\n+    if drift_warnings:\n+        print(\"Nodepack lockfile warnings:\")\n+        for warning in drift_warnings:\n+            print(f\"- {warning}\")\n     report = workflow.validate(schema_provider=schema_provider)\n@@\n def _read_doctor_lockfile() -> list[LockEntry]:\n     return read_lockfile()\n+\n+\n+def _nodepack_lockfile_drift() -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:\n+    warnings: list[str] = []\n+    errors: list[str] = []\n+    for entry in _read_doctor_lockfile():\n+        pack_dir = _doctor_nodepack_dir(entry.name)\n+        if pack_dir is None:\n+            warnings.append(f\"{entry.name} in lockfile but not installed; skipping drift check\")\n+            continue\n+        actual = _git_head(pack_dir)\n+        if actual is None:\n+            warnings.append(f\"{entry.name} is installed at {pack_dir} but git HEAD could not be read; skipping drift check\")\n+            continue\n+        if actual != entry.git_commit_sha:\n+            errors.append(f\"{entry.name} git HEAD {actual} does not match lockfile git_commit_sha {entry.git_commit_sha}\")\n+        for rel_path, expected_hash in entry.source_sha256.items():\n+            source_path = pack_dir / rel_path\n+            if not source_path.is_file():\n+                errors.append(f\"{entry.name} source {rel_path} is missing; expected sha256 {expected_hash}\")\n+                continue\n+            actual_hash = hashlib.sha256(source_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n+            if actual_hash != expected_hash:\n+                errors.append(\n+                    f\"{entry.name} source {rel_path} sha256 {actual_hash} does not match lockfile {expected_hash}\"\n+                )\n+    return warnings, errors\n+\n+\n+def _doctor_nodepack_dir(name: str) -> Path | None:\n+    candidates = (\n+        Path(\"vendor\") / name,\n+        Path(\"custom_nodes\") / name,\n+        Path(\"vendor\") / \"ComfyUI\" / \"custom_nodes\" / name,\n+    )\n+    for candidate in candidates:\n+        if candidate.is_dir():\n+            return candidate\n+    return None\n+\n+\n+def _git_head(pack_dir: Path) -> str | None:\n+    try:\n+        result = subprocess.run(\n+            [\"git\", \"-C\", str(pack_dir), \"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\"],\n+            check=True,\n+            capture_output=True,\n+            text=True,\n+        )\n+    except (OSError, subprocess.CalledProcessError):\n+        return None\n+    return result.stdout.strip() or None\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
P1 Foundation: typed Handle scaffold + custom-node lifecycle/lockfile + RunPod smoke-test scaffold.

This is the combined first phase of implementing the Python composition DSL designed in `docs/python_composition_dsl_plan.md` (recently revised — read the current version) and addressing the issues called out in `docs/python_composition_dsl_review.md`. It folds together two originally-separate efforts (MP-2 typed Handles, MP-4 lifecycle/lockfile) plus a RunPod-based smoke-test scaffold so the whole thing can ship as one squashable PR.

Two later phases will build on this output: (a) MP-3 will expand the block library and add the compile-time serialization gate; (b) MP-5 will introduce VibeFlow multi-stage orchestration. Do NOT preempt their scope.

DELIVERABLES — all must land:

### A. Typed Handle scaffold (closes review §1, §2, §3, §10)

A1. `Handle` dataclass — frozen, fields: `node_id` (str), `output_slot` (str | int), `output_type` (str | None — populated later by MP-6 schema integration), `name` (str | None).
   - `__str__` returns `f"{node_id}.{output_slot}"` matching the existing string-ref convention (see `vibecomfy/blocks/subgraph.py:47` precedent and `vibecomfy/workflow.py:128-132` consumer that splits on `.`).
   - `__eq__` matches BOTH another Handle (by node_id+slot) AND its string form, so `handle == "12.0"` works without manual `str()`. Closes the silent type-erasure hole.
   - `__hash__` consistent with `__eq__`.

A2. Tighten `Handles` (plural wrapper at `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:11-35`) — change value type from `Mapping[str, Any]` to `Mapping[str, Handle]`. Update construction sites in `vibecomfy/blocks/` (notably `subgraph.py`) to wrap string refs as Handle instances. Backward-compat: callers passing raw strings must be coerced with a `DeprecationWarning` once per call site.

A3. `wf.node(class_type, **kwargs)` — typed authoring wrapper over `VibeWorkflow.add_node(...)` (`vibecomfy/workflow.py:122-126`). Returns an object with `.out(name_or_index) -> Handle` and `.id` (matches GraphBuilder's `id=` convention). Per the revised plan's API Concepts, this is the canonical authoring entry point. Does NOT promise mypy-grade safety; `T` parameterization deferred to a later phase that commits to overloads or codegen.

A4. `wf.run_until(handle)` raise contract — implement the method signature, raise `NotImplementedError` with a clear message if `handle.output_type is None`. Documented now to prevent downstream code from forming against partial behavior before MP-6 ungates it.

A5. Lint/doctor coverage of `str(handle)` coercion — extend `vibecomfy doctor` (search `vibecomfy/cli/`) to flag:
   - Raw string refs in block code where a typed Handle is in scope (warning).
   - Sites where `str(handle)` flows into `connect()` or dict keys (warning).
   New rule name per plan §"Lint / Guardrail Ideas": `untyped_raw_ref`.

A6. Parity tests for at least 3 ready templates: build via `wf.node(...).out(...)`, assert `compile("api")` matches `compile("graphbuilder")` (modulo allowed-difference set: node IDs may move, MarkdownNote may be stripped). Use `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` as one anchor.

### B. Custom-node lifecycle and lockfile enforcement (closes review §6, §7)

B1. `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` — new explicit verb. Refuses while a `run()` is in flight (see B2), closes existing `_comfy` context (mirror `stop()` at session.py:185-195 including the `model_mmap_residency` AttributeError swallow), reopens fresh `_comfy` (mirror `start()` at session.py:103-109), resets cached state carrying stale `NODE_CLASS_MAPPINGS` references. Implement equivalent for `ServerSession` (tear down and respawn the comfyui serve subprocess).

B2. **In-flight guard** on `session.stop()` and `reload_for_nodepack_change()` — today `EmbeddedSession.stop()` (session.py:185-195) doesn't check if a `run()` is awaiting `queue_prompt_api`. Add a tracking field (e.g. `_inflight_run: asyncio.Future | None`) and have stop() either refuse with a clear `RuntimeError`, OR cancel-and-await. Audit current `session.stop()` call sites; if both options reasonable, expose `wait_for_inflight: bool = True`.

B3. **`ServerSession` external-mode handling** — when the session targets an external-managed `comfyui serve` (one VibeComfy didn't spawn), `reload_for_nodepack_change()` MUST NOT terminate it. Raise `ExternalServerRestartRequired` (new error class) with a clear message. Today no detection path or error class exists — both must be created.

B4. **`vibecomfy nodes lock` CLI command** — write `custom_nodes.lock` after resolving any `semantic_label` values. Schema per plan §"Custom Nodes — Pinning, Hashes, and Lockfiles":
   - `git_commit_sha` (required primary pin — Git commit SHA-1, immutable).
   - `semantic_label` (optional advisory, e.g. "v1.4.0", "main").
   - `source_sha256` (optional per-node-class content digest, hex).
   Today's lockfile records `(name, sha, url)` only — see entries for `ComfyUI-KJNodes`, `ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper`. Extend format and migrate existing entries. Preserve `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` rename with backward-compat read.

B5. **`vibecomfy doctor` SHA verification verb** — read lockfile before `session.start()` (or as separate doctor invocation); verify each installed nodepack's git HEAD matches `git_commit_sha`. If `source_sha256` present, verify the named class file's content hash. Default on mismatch: **FAIL CLOSED**. Provide `--allow-drift` opt-in to downgrade to warning.

B6. **Tests**: in-flight guard, reload verb (with fake nodepack), external-server detection (assert `ExternalServerRestartRequired` raised, external process untouched), lockfile round-trip, SHA verification (synthetic mismatch fails closed without `--allow-drift`, warns with it).

### C. RunPod smoke-test scaffold (new)

C1. Add `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` (or `tests/runpod/`, follow existing test layout). The test:
   - Uses the `runpod_lifecycle` Python package (already available in this workspace; check `pip show runpod_lifecycle` or look in the broader workspace for the package). The package provisions ephemeral RunPod GPU pods, runs work, and tears them down.
   - Provisions one pod (cheap GPU class — e.g. RTX 4090 or A40), installs vibecomfy on it, runs ONE representative ready template through the new `wf.node(...).out(...)` typed-Handle authoring path, submits via `EmbeddedSession.run(...)` after a `reload_for_nodepack_change()` call (to exercise B1 + B2 paths), asserts the run produces expected outputs, tears the pod down.
   - Marked `@pytest.mark.runpod` so it's OPT-IN — never runs in default `pytest` invocations or CI without explicit opt-in.
   - Documented in the relevant test README or `docs/`: how to run it (`pytest -m runpod`), what credentials are required (RunPod API key env var), expected wall-clock cost, and the safety contract that it tears down on success AND failure.

C2. **DO NOT EXECUTE the smoke test as part of this megaplan run.** Provisioning real GPU pods burns credentials and budget — the megaplan executor must not do this. The deliverable is the test code + docs; the user (or post-merge CI) runs it explicitly.

### CRITICAL CONSTRAINTS

- **Backward compatibility is non-negotiable.** All existing tests must pass unchanged. Existing blocks returning `Handles({"image": "12.0"})` must keep working; tightening must coerce-with-warning. Existing `custom_nodes.lock` must still load (handle `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` migration). Existing `session.stop()` callers must keep working.
- Do NOT implement the compile-time serialization gate (MP-3 territory).
- Do NOT implement schema validation, `/object_info` snapshot, or `SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY` (MP-6 territory).
- Do NOT implement VibeFlow, ExternalPythonNode, `wrap_api_dict`, multi-stage orchestration, or stage-aware run-dir naming / `flow_id` / `stage_index` in `RunResult` (MP-5 territory; SD-013 in the plan).
- Do NOT expand the block library beyond what's needed for parity tests (MP-3 territory).
- Hot-loading a nodepack into a running session WITHOUT reload is explicitly out of scope per the plan.
- The RunPod smoke test must NOT be invoked during the megaplan execute phase.

### ACCEPTANCE

- All existing tests pass unchanged.
- New parity tests pass for 3+ ready templates.
- `Handle` is importable from a clean public path.
- `wf.node(...).out(...)` returns a Handle that compiles cleanly through `compile("api")`.
- `wf.run_until(handle)` raises `NotImplementedError` until `output_type` is populated.
- Doctor flags at least one synthetic `str(handle)` coercion case as `untyped_raw_ref` warning.
- New tests for in-flight guard, reload verb, external-server detection, lockfile round-trip, SHA verification all pass.
- `vibecomfy nodes lock` produces a lockfile in the new schema; existing entries migrate cleanly.
- `vibecomfy doctor` fails closed on synthetic SHA drift; succeeds with `--allow-drift`.
- `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` is exposed and documented.
- `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` (or equivalent) exists, is marked `@pytest.mark.runpod`, contains the smoke flow described in C1, and is documented for opt-in execution. The test is NOT executed as part of acceptance — only its existence and shape are verified.

User notes and answers:
- USER DECISION on B3 (ServerSession external-mode): adopt option (C) — DROP B3 entirely from this MP.

Rationale verified by reading code:
1. vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:12-14 already protects external servers — comfy_server(server_url=...) yields the URL without spawning, and the cleanup at lines 26-32 only fires when 'process' is non-None (i.e. only for managed subprocesses).
2. ServerSession in current production code ALWAYS owns its own subprocess (start() always calls _spawn_comfy_server). There is no production codepath that creates a ServerSession attached to an external URL — vibecomfy/runtime/run.py bypasses ServerSession entirely when given server_url=.
3. Therefore reload_for_nodepack_change() on ServerSession is always safe to tear down its own subprocess without external-mode branching, and adding _external + ExternalServerRestartRequired now would be unreachable mechanism (exactly what review §0 'every documented invariant needs a code-level check' said NOT to do).

- All other deliverables (A1-A6, C1-C2) unchanged.

At the top of each class's `run()` method, before any other work: if self._inflight_run is not None and not self._inflight_run.done() \u2192 raise RuntimeError('session already has a run in flight; concurrent run() is not supported in P1'); then `self._inflight_run = asyncio.current_task()`. In a finally block at the end of run(): `self._inflight_run = None`. Wrap the entire body of run() so the tracker covers queue submit + watchdog finalization + metadata write. Modify EmbeddedSession.stop and ServerSession.stop to take `wait_for_inflight: bool = True`: if _inflight_run set + done \u2192 clear field, proceed with normal teardown; if set + not done + wait_for_inflight=True \u2192 await self._inflight_run, catch and re-raise the run's own exception before tearing down; if set + not done + wait_for_inflight=False \u2192 raise RuntimeError('session.stop() called while a run is in flight; pass wait_for_inflight=True or call after run completes'); no _inflight_run \u2192 existing behavior unchanged so current tests/test_runtime_session.py callers stay green.",

  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance and judgment calls:\n\nORDER MATTERS: T1 (Handle module) is the bedrock \u2014 every other phase-A task depends on it. Build T1 first and verify the import path works (`python -c \"from vibecomfy import Handle\"`) before touching anything else. Phase B is largely independent of Phase A and can be developed in parallel; T17 is the integration point.\n\nDOCTOR SEAM: The most subtle correctness item is the _read_doctor_lockfile() seam (T6) and the matching monkeypatch in T17 sub-step 6. The plan went through six iterations to converge on this design. Implement the seam as a top-level module function (not nested inside _cmd_doctor) so monkeypatch.setattr can target it. Recommend a shared autouse fixture in a tests/ conftest scoped to test_cli.py and test_doctor_models.py rather than per-test monkeypatch \u2014 cleaner and prevents drift if new doctor tests are added.\n\nBLOCK MIGRATION SWEEP: Run `grep -RIn 'Handles(' vibecomfy/blocks/` BEFORE editing T3, save the list, and check it off as you go. Easy to miss decode.py or video.py \u2014 both surfaced as iter-2 flag. The brief was explicit that the audit must be exhaustive.\n\nPARITY FIXTURES: Pre-pick anchors #2 and #3 before writing T7. flux2_klein_4b_t2i is required; suggest a Z-Image or SDXL variant + one audio/video template for structural variety. Document the choice in the test docstring so reviewers see the rationale.\n\nCONNECT SIGNATURE: For T4, vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py:connect (the helper) is called by block helpers, not user code. Preserve the legacy str + explicit output_slot kwarg branch; only the Handle-source branch is new. Don't break existing block helper callers.\n\nCONCURRENT RUN GUARD: T10's check-then-set sequence is racy if there's no lock, but Python asyncio's single-threaded event loop makes the check-and-set atomic between awaits. The guard is `if self._inflight_run is not None and not self._inflight_run.done(): raise RuntimeError(...)` followed immediately by `self._inflight_run = asyncio.current_task()` \u2014 no awaits between, so this is correct under asyncio semantics.\n\nRUNPOD CONFTEST: T18's pytest_collection_modifyitems must call BOTH `config.hook.pytest_deselected(items=deselected)` AND `items[:] = selected`. The hook reports deselection in pytest output ('=== N deselected ==='); the slice assignment is what actually removes them from the run. Skipping just the hook means tests still execute; skipping just the slice means deselection happens but isn't reported.\n\nDO NOT EXECUTE THE RUNPOD SMOKE TEST. The test scaffold's existence is the deliverable; running it provisions real GPU pods. T19's verdict is satisfied by file presence + structure, not by a green run.\n\nPR SHAPE: This will be a large diff \u2014 handles.py (new) + 8 blocks files + workflow.py + session.py + 2 commands + node_packs_lockfile.py (new) + ~10 test files + 2 docs. Tag the PR body with deliverable IDs A1\u2013A6, B1, B2, B4, B5, B6, C1\u2013C2 so reviewers can map sections to the brief.\n\nDEFERRED ITEMS (do not implement): MP-3 compile-time serialization gate, expanded block library; MP-5 VibeFlow, ExternalPythonNode, wrap_api_dict, flow_id/stage_index in RunResult, ServerSession external-mode handling; MP-6 object_info snapshot, SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY, named-output-to-slot resolution.",
